## 2. CLI Layer

function/folder: src/cli/
usage: Contains related domain logic and modules for src/cli/.
example:
```typescript
// Internal structure mapping
```

function/folder: src/cli/commands/
usage: Contains related domain logic and modules for src/cli/commands/.
example:
```typescript
// Internal structure mapping
```

function/folder: src/cli/commands/build.js
usage: `build` CLI command — builds a PPTX from a template + JSON data.
example:
```typescript
import * as mod from "./src/cli/commands/build.js";
```

function/folder: src/cli/commands/debug.js
usage: `debug` CLI command — diagnoses and repairs corrupted PPTX files.
example:
```typescript
import * as mod from "./src/cli/commands/debug.js";
```

function/folder: src/cli/commands/extract.js
usage: `extract` CLI command — extracts XML parts from a PPTX.
example:
```typescript
import * as mod from "./src/cli/commands/extract.js";
```

function/folder: src/cli/commands/inspect.js
usage: `inspect` CLI command — detailed PPTX structure inspection.
